<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many minutes should it take the train to travel from Wolverhampton to Milton Keynes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken} &= 10{:}10 - 8{:}40 \\\\
                  &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                  &= 60 \ \text {minutes} + 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                  &= 60 + 30 \\\\
                  &= 90 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$90 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken} &= 10{:}10 - 8{:}40 \\\\
                  &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                  &= 60 \ \text {minutes} + 30 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                  &= 60 + 30 \\\\
                  &= 90 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>

                    &= 10{:}38 \ \text{a.m}
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-EDEC9010-CA4E-4456-BF0A-E30E81B8226D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Thrse are the start and finish times on a DVD recorder:

$\bold{START\:\:\:\: 14{:}48}$

$\bold{FINISH\:\:\:\: 17{:}25}$

For how long was the video recording? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Length of recording} &= 17{:}25 - 14{:}48 \\\\
                                  &= 2 \ \text {hours} \ 37 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-DBC1958C-C043-4319-9AE7-09CEA696EBC9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $463 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes ?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 463 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac {463} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 7 \dfrac {43} {60} \\\\
&= 7 \ \text{hours} \ 43 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7 \ \text{hours} \ 43 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 463 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac {463} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 7 \dfrac {43} {60} \\\\
&= 7 \ \text{hours} \ 43 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7 \ \text{hours} \ 43 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FFA405AA-B004-43FE-A6D8-72C925702C2D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Melanie’s father took $1 \ \text{hr} \ 25 \ \text{mins}$ to drive from Peterborough to Oxford. He left Peterborough at $11.45  \ \text{am}$. At what time did he arrive in Oxford? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Arrival Time}           &= 11{:}45   + 1 \ \text{hours} \ 25 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                               &= 1{:}10 \ \text {pm} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1:10 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Arrival Time}           &= 11{:}45   + 1 \ \text{hours} \ 25 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                               &= 1{:}10 \ \text {pm} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1:10 \ \text{pm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-154BD462-2E71-4EBE-AD0B-A1A972F20268'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Change $4.6 \ \text{minutes}$ into seconds. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4.6 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 4.6 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 276 \  \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$276 \ \text{seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}

&= 4.6 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 276 \  \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4EB55D89-BF4F-47CA-A07F-BC5A04143A7B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$2008$ is a leap year, and this year the $29 \text{th}$ of February falls on a Friday. On what day of the week will the $29 \text{th}$ of February fall in the next leap year  $(2012)$ ? (Remember there are $366$ days in a leap year).

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$\text {Wednesday}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\text {Wednesday}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\text {Wednesday}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\text {Wednesday}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $387 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 387 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac{387} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 6 \dfrac {27} {60} \\\\
&= 6 \ \text{hours} \ 27 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6 \ \text{hours} \ 27 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 387 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac{387} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 6 \dfrac {27} {60} \\\\
&= 6 \ \text{hours} \ 27 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3568C8D0-C035-4738-8BE0-B0BC876E29B4'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A boy has to be in school by $8.35 \ \text{am}$. It takes him $25 \ \text{minutes}$ to get washed and dressed, $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to eat breakfast and then $18 \ \text{minutes}$ to walk to school.What is the latest time that he should get out of bed?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}  &= 8{:}35  - 25 \ \text{minutes} - 15 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 8{:}35  - 58 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 7{:}37 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7{:}37 \ \text{am}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}  &= 8{:}35  - 25 \ \text{minutes} - 15 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 8{:}35  - 58 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 7{:}37 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7{:}37 \ \text{am}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-27309122-5511-4842-BA1A-FDCDE985F81B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

You arrive at a bus stop $18 \ \text{minutes}$ late. The buses run every $45 \ \text{minutes}$. How long do you have to wait for the next bus? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting Time} &= 45 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 27 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$27 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting Time} &= 45 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 27 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0F642196-79F1-4CFA-BF49-1BD4515A47BD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

You have to catch the bus after school. Buses leave school at $15.55$ and $16.35$. If you are $4 \ \text{minutes}$ late for the first bus, how long do you have to wait for the second bus?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting Time} &= 16.35 - 15.55 - 4 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 40 \ \text{minutes} - 4 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 36 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$36 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting Time} &= 16.35 - 15.55 - 4 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 40 \ \text{minutes} - 4 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 36 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$36 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-219060A7-EC1A-4ED0-B135-50542C15C7B7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Antony is making some cupcakes. It takes him $20 \ \text{minutes}$ to prepare the ingredients, the cakes take $22 \ \text{minutes}$ to bake and he needs to leave them for $10 \ \text{minutes}$ to cool down. If he wants to eat them at 4:10pm, what time should he start preparing the ingredients?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Start Time}               &= 4{:}10  - 20 \ \text{minutes} - 22 \ \text{minutes} - 10 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 4{:}10 - 52 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 3{:}18 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3{:}18 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Start Time}               &= 4{:}10  - 20 \ \text{minutes} - 22 \ \text{minutes} - 10 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 4{:}10 - 52 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 3{:}18 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3{:}18 \ \text{pm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B72AF037-E752-4BED-91CE-38848837F037'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Subtract the number of $\text{hours}$ in a day from the number of $\text{seconds}$ in a $\text{minute}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text{Number of seconds in a minute} - \text{Number of hours in a day} \\\\
&= 60 - 24 \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text{Number of seconds in a minute} - \text{Number of hours in a day} \\\\
&= 60 - 24 \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$36$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-434CD114-4752-47E2-A24B-B5229509F3BD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Tasweer is making some cupcakes. It takes her $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to prepare the ingredients, the cakes take $18 \ \text{minutes}$ to bake and she needs to leave them for $10 \ \text{minutes}$ to cool down. If she wants to eat them at $3:15 \ \text{pm}$, what time should she start preparing the ingredients? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Start Time}                                           &= 3{:}15  - 15 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} - 10 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                            &= 3{:}15 - 43 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                            &= 2{:}32 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2{:}32 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Start Time}                                           &= 3{:}15  - 15 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} - 10 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                            &= 3{:}15 - 43 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                            &= 2{:}32 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2{:}32 \ \text{pm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-535DDC7C-F46C-4FFD-AF90-E1FC89C96B9F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many $\text{minutes}$ are there in one day? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{day} \\\\
&= 1 \times 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 1440 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1440 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{day} \\\\
&= 1 \times 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 1440 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-33697C4D-2660-4DBB-9DF6-4265704C84DB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Josh has to be at work at $8:15 \ \text{am}$. It takes him $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to get dressed, $20 \ \text{minutes}$ to eat breakfast and $12 \ \text{minutes}$ to drive to work. What is the latest time Josh could get up to get to work on time?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Josh should get up}                                         &= \text{Office time} - (\text{Time to get dressed + Time to eat + Time to drink}) \\\\
                                                                       &= 8{:}15 \ \text{am} - (15 + 20 + 12) \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                                       &= 8{:}15 \ \text{am} - 47 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                                       &= 9{:}02 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

$9{:}02 \ \text{am}$

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-71A87121-8B24-43F3-8CA2-5883E0DFF79B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Ann went to the cinema. The film started at $11{:}55 \ \text{AM}$ and lasted $117$ minutes. What time did the film end?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Film ended}   &= 11{:}55  + 117 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 11{:}55 + 1 \ \text {hours} \ 57 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 12{:}55 + 57 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 01{:}52 \ \text {pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$01{:}52 \ \text {pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Film ended}   &= 11{:}55  + 117 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 11{:}55 + 1 \ \text {hours} \ 57 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 12{:}55 + 57 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 01{:}52 \ \text {pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CB718008-6C5B-486B-83F4-A32A6530AAA0'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

My train was scheduled to leave at $17{:}40$ and to arrive at $18{:}20$. However it started $5  \ \text{minutes}$ late and the journey then took $42  \ \text{minutes}$. At what time did I arrive?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time I Arrived} \ &= \ \text{Times scheduled} + \text{Late} + \text{Time of journey} \\\\
                        &= 17{:}40 + 5 \ \text{min} + 42 \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 17{:}40 + 47 \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 17{:}40 + (20 + 27) \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 18{:}00 + 27 \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 18{:}27
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$18{:}27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time I Arrived} \ &= \ \text{Times scheduled} + \text{Late} + \text{Time of journey} \\\\
                        &= 17{:}40 + 5 \ \text{min} + 42 \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 17{:}40 + 47 \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 17{:}40 + (20 + 27) \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 18{:}00 + 27 \ \text{min} \\\\
                        &= 18{:}27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$18{:}27$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B436492B-ADE1-4E62-B5CC-10E5C7D215FC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many seconds are there in $\dfrac {1} {4} \ \text{of} \ \dfrac {1} {6} \ \text{of} \  \dfrac {1} {8}$ of a day?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

We know, $1$ day has $24$ hours, $1$ hour has $60$ minutes and $1$ minute has $60$ seconds. 

$1$ day = $(24 \times 60 \times 60)$ seconds.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text {of} \ \dfrac {1} {6} \ \text {of} \ \dfrac {1} {8} \ \text {of} \ 1 \ \text {day} \\\\ 
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times  \dfrac {1} {6} \times  \dfrac {1} {8} \times 24 \times 60 \times 60 \\\\\
&= \dfrac {24 \times 60 \times 60} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 4 \times 15 \times 6 \times 10} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 3 \times \cancel {4} \times 15 \times \cancel {6} \times 10} {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 15 \times 10 \\\\
&= 45 \times 10 \\\\
&= 450 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$450 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>We know, $1$ day has $24$ hours, $1$ hour has $60$ minutes and $1$ minute has $60$ seconds. 

$1$ day = $(24 \times 60 \times 60)$ seconds.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text {of} \ \dfrac {1} {6} \ \text {of} \ \dfrac {1} {8} \ \text {of} \ 1 \ \text {day} \\\\ 
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times  \dfrac {1} {6} \times  \dfrac {1} {8} \times 24 \times 60 \times 60 \\\\\
&= \dfrac {24 \times 60 \times 60} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 4 \times 15 \times 6 \times 10} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 3 \times \cancel {4} \times 15 \times \cancel {6} \times 10} {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 15 \times 10 \\\\
&= 45 \times 10 \\\\
&= 450 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C85A7D1C-3318-4EEB-988F-C687D36512BB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many minutes are there from $11{:}11$ until $23{:}23$ on the same day?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
&= 12 \ \text{hours} \ 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 720 + 12 \\\\
&= 732 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$732 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
&= 12 \ \text{hours} \ 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 720 + 12 \\\\
&= 732 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FBA0759C-897A-43DA-B30F-45535D9B82AC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many seconds are there in $8 \ \text{minutes}$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times 60 \\\\
&= 480 \ \text{ seconds }
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$480 \ \text{ seconds }$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times 60 \\\\
&= 480 \ \text{ seconds }
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$480 \ \text{ seconds }$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6706DEBD-210D-4CFE-8AFB-1AA7E39ACD32'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

There are $329$ days until my birthday. How many weeks is this? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {329} {7} \\\\
&=47 \ \text {Weeks}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$47 \ \text {Weeks}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {329} {7} \\\\
&=47 \ \text {Weeks}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$47 \ \text {Weeks}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6600A0C0-CEAE-4BC5-A600-7F98F7F3604B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A train leaves Solihull at $6.35 \ \text{am}$ and arrives at Liverpool at $10.22 \ \text{am}$. How many minutes does it take? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}     &= 10{:}22 - 6{:}35 \\\\
                      &= 3 \ \text{hour} \ 47 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 3 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 47 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 180 + 47 \\\\
                      &= 227  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$



</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$227  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}     &= 10{:}22 - 6{:}35 \\\\
                      &= 3 \ \text{hour} \ 47 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 3 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 47 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 180 + 47 \\\\
                      &= 227  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$


</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$227  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

How many seconds are there in $21 \ \text{minutes}$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 21 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 1260 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1260 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>

\begin{aligned}

&= 1260 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A20253DA-6F06-46F2-A737-C228987D5A92'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jacqueline needs to catch a flight early tomorrow morning. She needs to get out of bed at $3.15 \ \text{am}$, but wants to make sure that she has at least $7\dfrac {1} {2}$ hours of sleep. What is the
                                
latest time that Jacqueline can go to sleep this evening? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {latest time she can go to sleep} &= 3.15 \ \text{am} - 7\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {hours} \\\\
                                        &= 3:15 \ \text{am} - 7 \ \text {hours} \ 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                        &= 8:46 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8:46 \ \text{am}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {latest time she can go to sleep} &= 3.15 \ \text{am} - 7\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {hours} \\\\
                                        &= 3:15 \ \text{am} - 7 \ \text {hours} \ 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                        &= 8:46 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8:46 \ \text{am}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

An aeroplane leaves Heathrow at $$21{:}26$$ and arrives at Atlanta at $$10{:}45$$ on the following day. How long does this journey take, in hours and minutes?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}           &= 21{:}26 - 10{:}45 \\\\
                            &= 13 \ \text{hours} \ 19 \ \text{minutes} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13 \ \text{hours} \ 19 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}           &= 21{:}26 - 10{:}45 \\\\
                            &= 13 \ \text{hours} \ 19 \ \text{minutes} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

I start watching a film at $6:55\,pm$ and turn the television off at $9:35\,pm$.  

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

For how long was I watching television? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9{:}35 \ \text{am} - 6{:}55 \ \text{am} \\\\
&= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 30 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
&= 2 \times 60 + 30  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 120 + 30  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 150  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$150  \ \text{minutes}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9{:}35 \ \text{am} - 6{:}55 \ \text{am} \\\\
&= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 30 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
&= 2 \times 60 + 30  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 120 + 30  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 150  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$150  \ \text{minutes}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F541C565-A23A-42AB-A949-6DABF9981E5A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

I start watching a film at $7:45\,pm$ and turn the television off at $9:35\,pm$. For how long was I watching television?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time left}    &= 9{:}35 \ \text{am} - 7{:}45 \ \text{am} \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 55 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                    &= 60 + 55  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 115  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$115  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time left}    &= 9{:}35 \ \text{am} - 7{:}45 \ \text{am} \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 55 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                    &= 60 + 55  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 115  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$115  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

step 3 : write 1 x 60 minutes + ....

add a step 4 : = 60 + 55


</div>

How many minutes does the train’s journey from Brooklyn Bridge to $125^{th}$ Street take?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}     &= 12{:}35 - 12{:}08 \\\\
                      &= 27  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question question'>

A man starts work at $08:15$. He then has a break from $10:15$ to $10:35$. After his break he continues working until $12:50$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

For how many hours and minutes was he working?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total Working Hours} &= \text{End time - Start time - Break} \\\\
                           &= 12:50 - 08:15 - (10:35 - 10:15)
                           &= 12 {:} 50 - 8 {:} 15 - 20 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &= 4 {:} 35 - 20 mins\ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &= 4 {:} 15 \\\\
                           &= 4 \ \text{hour} 15 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4 \ \text{hour} 15 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total Working Hours} &= \text{End time - Start time - Break} \\\\
                           &= 12:50 - 08:15 - (10:35 - 10:15)
                           &= 12 {:} 50 - 8 {:} 15 - 20 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &= 4 {:} 35 - 20 mins\ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &= 4 {:} 15 \\\\
                           &= 4 \ \text{hour} 15 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$4 \ \text{hour} 15 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If he is paid $\pounds 6.00$ for each hour worked, how much will he be paid for all the work?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total (Paid)} &= \text{Total Hours worked} \times \text{per hour pay} \\\\
                     &= 4 \ \text{hours} \ 15 \ \text{ minutes} \times  \pounds 6 \\\\
                     &= 4 \ \text{hours} \times 6 + \dfrac {1} {4} \times 6 \\\\
                     &= 24 + 2 \times \dfrac {3} {2} \times 2 \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2} } {2 \times \cancel {2}}  \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
                     &= 24 + 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \pounds 1.50 \\\\
                     &= \pounds 25.5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 25.5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total (Paid)} &= \text{Total Hours worked} \times \text{per hour pay} \\\\
                     &= 4 \ \text{hours} \ 15 \ \text{ minutes} \times  \pounds 6 \\\\
                     &= 4 \ \text{hours} \times 6 + \dfrac {1} {4} \times 6 \\\\
                     &= 24 + 2 \times \dfrac {3} {2} \times 2 \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2} } {2 \times \cancel {2}}  \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
                     &= 24 + 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \pounds 1.50 \\\\
                     &= \pounds 25.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 25.5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>
